Ink O'Clock — public-domain clock lines

These sentences come from works that are free to copy in Canada under the
rule requested for this gather. Canada moved from life-plus-50 to life-plus-70
on 30 December 2022. Works that were already free stayed free. An author who
died in 1971 or earlier was already in the public domain, so the
work is included. A later or unknown death is not. Translators get the same test.

Sources: Project Gutenberg, Standard Ebooks, English Wikisource, Faded Page,
and public-domain lines from older books. A translation is kept only when
the translator also died in 1971 or earlier. English Wikisource pages and
Gazette issues are kept only when the copyright has expired. A short timetable
line is kept when it states a.m. or p.m. British Library digitised books
(1500s to 1899) are kept when every listed author died in 1971 or earlier,
or the book has no author, or it was printed in 1880 or earlier.
Hansard lines are not public domain;
each one is copied under the Open Parliament Licence.
Original lines were written for this clock. They borrow a voice, such as a
pirate or a starship log, and they are not quotations from those films or books.
The clock phrase is wrapped in « ». A first-pass line with no morning or
evening stays under "Time of day not stated". A gap-fill line is placed
on the hour the sentence names.

Lines with a time of day: 2490
Lines without a time of day: 2063

00:03

↑ ["To-morrow is my birthday—that is to say, at twelve o' the clock, midnight; i.e . in twelve minutes I shall have completed thirty and three years of age!!! and I go to my bed with a heaviness of heart at having lived so long, and to so little purpose. * * * It is «three minutes past twelve»—''Tis the middle of night by the castle clock,' and I am now thirty-three!—
— The Works of Lord Byron (ed. Coleridge, Prothero)/Poetry/Volume 7/On my Thirty-third Birthday, George Gordon Byron (died 1824)
Wikisource: https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/The_Works_of_Lord_Byron_%28ed._Coleridge%2C_Prothero%29/Poetry/Volume_7/On_my_Thirty-third_Birthday
